The tracked browser manifest is not-packaged (build-time resource staging may replace it); C6.2B5 hosted installer passed at predecessor 48250a2e, not proof of live Google search on the current build. Actual browser availability and provider results need physical proof. |
| P0 explicit path not honored | lib.rs L4985–4999 and [workspace_desktop.rs L218–238, L331–348](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/blob/25618e383dda49f0cc85a92ef6f6f0b745c378ea/lu-desktop/src-tauri/src/workspace_desktop.rs#L331-L348) force a fresh OS folder chooser instead of resolving the explicit original human path as a bounded proposed resource. Preserve explicit authorization and reparse/symlink/root guards; use existing authorized roots or native confirmation of exact named resource, not ambient file access. Clarify only unresolved paths. |
| P0 inconsistent document intake | [main.jsx L4161–4294](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/blob/25618e383dda49f0cc85a92ef6f6f0b745c378ea/lu-desktop/web/main.jsx#L4161-L4294) has separate native Reader extraction and browser drop/paste routines; L4293 explicitly rejects PDF/DOCX; L6269 file input excludes them. [native_reader.rs L13–29](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/blob/25618e383dda49f0cc85a92ef6f6f0b745c378ea/src-tauri/lu-core/src/native_reader.rs#L13-L29) already lists PDF, DOCX, XLSX, PPTX, ODT, EPUB etc. Do not duplicate parsing in WebView. One typed, host-owned attachment admission path with supported formats/limits, immutable source identity and safe extraction; no path or prompt text as authority. |
| P0 image transaction and display defect | [main.jsx L4079–4110](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/blob/25618e383dda49f0cc85a92ef6f6f0b745c378ea/lu-desktop/web/main.jsx#L4079-L4110) clears draft and renders an optimistic text-only message before host acceptance; [L4150–4159](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/blob/25618e383dda49f0cc85a92ef6f6f0b745c378ea/lu-desktop/web/main.jsx#L4150-L4159) restores only text on failure and unconditionally revokes previews/releases refs. L4375–4435 renders saved text without attachment chips. [lib.rs L5116–5185](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/blob/25618e383dda49f0cc85a92ef6f6f0b745c378ea/lu-desktop/src-tauri/src/lib.rs#L5116-L5185) resolves ephemeral image refs and journals prompt text; L5835–5855 selects native image inference when refs arrive. Backend has an image path, but user-visible persistence/retry and actual physical MMProj result remain unverified. Test async ingestion vs Send, chat switch/new chat, cancellation, restart, multi-image, model eject and no cross-chat/egress. |
| P0 execution architecture fractured | [main.jsx L4021–4110](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/blob/25618e383dda49f0cc85a92ef6f6f0b745c378ea/lu-desktop/web/main.jsx#L4021-L4110) branches by modes and fixed 512-token sentinel; [lib.rs L4704–5058](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/blob/25618e383dda49f0cc85a92ef6f6f0b745c378ea/lu-desktop/src-tauri/src/lib.rs#L4704-L5058) repeats CPU classifier and chains exact-phrase early returns; actual fallback calls supervisor.run_reserved_with_history / run_reserved_images_with_history at L5835–5855, not the common supervised tool-generation loop. This is not proof that no tools work: specialized lanes have real broker/registry infrastructure. But ordinary chat has no demonstrated general discovery -> proposed tool -> consent -> execution -> feedback -> next step path across tools. [native_capability_inventory.rs](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/blob/25618e383dda49f0cc85a92ef6f6f0b745c378ea/src-tauri/lu-core/src/native_capability_inventory.rs) projects only registered, codec-matched, already-granted tools, explicitly not verified dependencies; [lib.rs L9188–9269](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/blob/25618e383dda49f0cc85a92ef6f6f0b745c378ea/lu-desktop/src-tauri/src/lib.rs#L9188-L9269) also maintains a separate static route catalog. #### Design and measurement guardrails

Use [canonical execution-layer philosophy](LU_AI_EXECUTION_LAYER_PHILOSOPHY_20261107.md): **C_practical = f(K,I,A,F)**; K = knowledge/reasoning, I = real environmental interfaces, A = user-governed authorized resource/effect, F = feedback/independent verification/recovery. User defines purpose; effective knowledge includes source-bound memory. Treat this as conceptual, not a weighted score. Model does not grant itself authority. Present truthful capability availability even before authorization; grant exact action only after applicable policy and consent. Benchmark improvement against equivalent authorizations in tool-enabled comparison arms; model-only baseline cannot claim external effects. Acceptance requires correct independently witnessed outcomes, zero unauthorized effects and no regression of original controls; report observed efficiency/quality tradeoffs rather than asserting speedup.

**Next actionable task: CP-01 transactional attachment lifecycle, then group CP-02–04 and CP-05–06 as evidence-driven repairs; CP-07/08 may proceed alongside once contracts are pinned.** Complete CP-09 benchmark and CP-10 consolidated acceptance before calling any subsequent build ready. No runtime modifications or CI dispatch in this documentation checkpoint.

### 2026-11-09 — IDE-05.3 narrow protected package-worker integration

The Lu standalone IDE's manager-of-managers advanced from IDE-05.2
static review to an **explicitly opt-in, offline and locked uv Sync**
protected native job using the *existing* WO6/D3 supervisor (not a
new process launcher). The native Tauri command takes a registered
project ID and exact preview/evidence SHA only; the host reconstructs
uv Sync, revalidates all manifest/lock observations before/after a fresh
45-second, cancellation-aware OS-native human dialog, seals an
immutable source tree, checks exact approved uv + Python runtime pins,
and binds the preview SHA into the one-use D3 JobPlan effect class.
Linux's existing delegated cgroup/Bubblewrap worker has no network,
read-only source and privately writable environment/cache. The Windows
worker currently **DENIES uv Sync** before native launch, since
immutable workspace ACLs are not yet proven; earlier Windows
AppContainer evidence alone does not suffice. Existing cancellation,
kernel descendant cleanup, journal quarantine and OS-only recovery are
reused. Add/Remove, Conan, vcpkg and online dependency resolution are
**NOT implemented for execution**. All native Windows/Linux compile,
human approval, crash/recovery and installed tests remain OPEN.
No mini-CI jobs dispatched; consolidate after IDE-05.4/05.5.

| **FIN-03 and Git 6A–6F** | The divergent `ide/fin03-6e05-protected-remote-preflight-20261113` tree has **zero files absent** from #236; all ten distinct Git implementation files (`git_conflict_resolution`, `git_partial_stage`, `git_protected_divergent_merge`, `git_protected_merge`, `git_protected_replay`, `git_reviewed_commit`, `git_reviewed_restore`, `git_reviewed_stash_save`, `git_reviewed_unstage`, `github_remote_publication_intent`) have **identical blob SHAs in #236**. | **Already integrated source**; no separate merge of PR #230, no replacement of current host implementation. Check original native recovery tests for genuine same-path regressions only. |
| **DEV-01 editor/navigation/drafts** | Old `source_index.rs`, `draft_recovery.rs`, `project_search_port.rs`, `quick_open.js` absent by name; current #236 has newer `quick_open.rs`, `draft_vault.rs`, `project_search.rs`, `source_file_transactions.rs`, `quick_open_view.js`. Multiple original DEV-01 `dev01_*contract.test.mjs` files, host index/draft tests and Linux installed fixture are absent. | **Superseded runtime source**; only potentially unique *negative/installed regression tests* should be translated onto current APIs. Never reintroduce a competing editor, project index or draft store. |
| **P05 buffer references/search** | Original `draft_ref_port.js` is absent, but **all seven exported function names** are present in current `buffer_identity.js` plus three new helpers; old `project_search_port.rs` and current `project_search.rs` both export bounded `find_in_project`. | **Functional source represented by modern successor**; keep only uncovered original acceptance contracts. |
| **DEV-02 test-only native LSP** | Original `stage_test_only_lsp_resources.py`, test, technical-only Tauri config and installed LSP fixtures are absent; current `stage_native_lsp_disposable_test_install.py` and `stage_complete_native_lsp_candidate.py` exist. | **Possible unique test coverage**, NOT missing production LSP. Review original negative/installed cases, retain only distinct ones in current packaging. |
| **FIN-04 / DEV-04** | Original diverged FIN-04 has absent `windows_fin04w_bridge.rs`, sometimes `windows_fin04w2_host.rs`, and old `Test-FIN04W2OriginalApprovedNative.ps1` / `Invoke-FIN04WDeniedOperationTrace.ps1`. The new two-SCM AppContainer/LLDB path, independent owner event and LLVM/PDB oracle are already in #236. | **Legacy bridge is intentionally superseded/excluded** under the creator's independent two-process design. Carry over only distinct original adversarial-test criteria; unfinished FIN-04 stays disabled, and completing FIN-04 is NOT the next integration priority. |
| **P06 release provenance** | **Concrete distinct absent verification source:** `ide/packaging/p06_shipping_content.py` implements bounded read-only `capture` / `verify` for a supplied shipping payload; `p06_live_native_jobs.py` and corresponding tests are absent. Current P06 license/lock/evidence modules exist, but not this exact source entrypoint. | **P1 unique verification-function candidate** for IDE-MERGE-02: check functional overlap with newer P06 release-verification scripts, then integrate genuinely distinct functions/tests only. Not installed/signed/human acceptance. |
| **Historical Wave C/D/E, WO4–WO9, P03/P04** | Sampled old Wave/WO tree files are already represented by current runtime paths, with historic revisions and old test-only scripts (e.g. `wo8_linux_wm_session.sh`, installed buffer smoke) absent. | **Superseded source**. Audit original installed/negative test requirements without mass historical PR replay or restoring unsafe workers. |
| **WO10 optional Lu bridge [#76](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/pull/76)** | `ide/bridge-contract/*`, `ide/proto/lu/ide/v1/bridge.proto`, `ide/host/src/bridge_policy.rs`, `bridge_wire.rs` absent; historical PR explicitly says *POST-LU CORE v1.0 / DEFERRED / DO NOT MERGE*. | **Excluded** (not missing approved IDE functionality). No Lu Core coupling or network pairing. |
| **Queue-pruning scripts** | `ide/scripts/prune_superseded_queued_actions.py`, `prune_superseded_repo_actions.py`, `test_repo_queue_hygiene.py` absent. | Operational hygiene only; no product functionality. Do not erase native CI evidence or make this a new integration branch. |

**Result and scope:** All branch/PR metadata and exact-head ancestry are enumerated. No genuinely missing *essential user-facing* IDE feature is proved by these old detached branches; the observed truly absent source is principally optional excluded bridge, superseded execution, original test/QA and P06 read-only shipping evidence. **Full semantic equivalence of every changed same-path file on all 60 diverged heads remains OPEN** and is explicitly delegated to IDE-MERGE-01/02 (existing path/function level), not treated as 60 new feature projects. The original consolidated Windows/Linux run [#43623320336](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/actions/runs/43623320336) **failed** at frozen #236 SHA; this is not release acceptance. Do not run new CI until coherent reviewed integration group. No new PR or branch.

**Known source-graph and test evidence:** [#236 IDE Group 6C–6F integration](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/pull/236) and its [branch-local reconciliation](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/blob/ide/integration-git-6c6d6e6f-to-main-20261113/ide/docs/IDE_GROUP6F_MAIN_CONVERGENCE_20261113.md). Original Group 6F run #43560518279 failed E0618, replacement #43560834931 failed remote-reconciliation fixture, and corresponding repairs were prepared; neither is a passing final receipt. Original 6D marker tests and 6E pull-review tests require separate reconciliation. The FIN-04W2-C4 Windows Tauri workflow failed compilation. Historical positive C2/C3 tests do **not** establish C4 or physical authorization. These are **integration backlog facts**, not reasons to stop source convergence.

**Handoff rule for every new IDE conversation:** begin with this section, reobserve `main`, #236, all source heads that moved, and the IDE branch/PR inventory. Report what unique functionality has actually landed in `main` versus only existing on drafts. Prioritize the earliest unfinished IDE-MERGE task, never invent a fresh independent task board, and never create another branch/PR simply because conversation context changed.

---

### Current priority — CORE-MERGE single-main convergence (November 13, 2026)

**Strategic execution-layer correction (owner recollection reaffirmed, 2026-11-13):** Preserve the November 7 [canonical AI Execution Layer philosophy](LU_AI_EXECUTION_LAYER_PHILOSOPHY_20261107.md) and [tool audit](LU_AI_EXECUTION_TOOL_REVIEW_20261107.md): **model intelligence + usable interfaces + user/host authority + real feedback**, not a growing count of domain-specific native tools. The Qwen document/spreadsheet experiment was illustrative evidence that effective task framing, selecting a fitting interface and recovering from an observed failure matter; it was **not** a general success benchmark. Reader/OCR, image utilities, browser, files, archive, typed execution and native tools already comprise sufficient source-level breadth for current Core consolidation. Future broad tool/module work is **DEFERRED BY DEFAULT**, even if an older branch contains absent files. A general JSON Schema utility, old environment-discovery command, second OCR fallback or second loop controller is **not** an automatic P0 development task: first show a user-task failure unhandled by the existing tool/execution stack, and only then consider a small audited primitive. Do not restore or reference the excluded legacy ANSF side adapter.

**Core convergence order remains C1→C3→C4→C5**, but the *functional emphasis* is the end-to-end **goal → relevant ANSF/task context → capability/prerequisite discovery → model plan and tool choice → exact current human/host grant → OS-supervised tool or app execution → independently observed result → verified completion or safe replan/recovery**. Favor exposing proven existing operations to the ordinary GGUF desktop workflow over building another image/Office/JSON micro-tool. Treat process/API/GUI execution as separately reviewed, limited effect surfaces (never ambient arbitrary shell, background permissions or model self-authorization). Evaluate on representative Qwen tasks with original artifacts, real tool-use choices, failure recovery and no invented success. Respect batch acceptance after a coherent source-integration group; do not start per-bug CI or a new branch/PR merely to pursue this correction. This strategic scope **supersedes** any historical next-action text below that prematurely ranks a new general-purpose validator or other domain tool above execution-layer consolidation.

**Authoritative execution backlog.** This section supersedes earlier historical tool-by-tool queue assumptions where functionality is already inherited in current Core source. The detailed, exact-head [Lu Core branch/PR inventory](LU_CORE_CONSOLIDATION_INVENTORY_20261113.md) is the source-graph receipt and must be reobserved if tips move. `main` is the canonical development index, not evidence that draft PRs have merged.

**Snapshot:** [CORE-01E PR #226](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/pull/226) at `f4beaba3f6e77693f9048cd7e67764142d6c41d2`; `main` at `2e31b22a597c8a92aec979d9ff057401c518088c`. GitHub shows **215 open PRs** (121 Core, 94 separately-owned IDE) and 286 live branches. All **158 Core branch heads** were compared with CORE-01E: **103 ancestral, 1 identical, 1 descendant, 53 divergent**. Of the 121 open Core PRs: **84 ancestral, 1 identical, 1 descendant and 35 divergent**. An ancestral branch head is inherited by Core, **not** merged into `main` or accepted for release.

| ID | Execution order / deliverable | Actual status | Next proof or action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **CORE-MERGE-C0** | Maintain complete exact-head PR/branch inventory; track IDE, P7L/KoboldCpp and optional P6 separately | **INVENTORIED / SOURCE TOPOLOGY VERIFIED** at frozen snapshot | Refresh comparisons on branch motion; never equate ancestry with accepted binaries |

Adapt a separate pure/read-only tool into the existing native registry/typed codec/authority; do **not** substitute the internal JSON boundary for general schema evaluation or copy incompatible old Protobuf/Cargo wholesale. |
| **Environment discovery (#17)** | `environment_context.rs` already provides fresh OS/CPU/GPU diagnostic hints. `compute_inventory.rs`, `native_capability_inventory.rs`, `native_app_catalog.rs` and `native_app_admission.rs` provide tighter capability and host-enrolled executable identity checks, with reviewed `native_app_version_execution.rs` for actual authorized version probes. Historical `HostProgramInventory` merely probed presence of host-selected candidate files from 12 fixed program IDs, returning `file_present_only`; its `lu_check_env` model tool is not in current Core. | **PARTIALLY SUPERSEDED; NO WHOLESALE IMPORT.** The only narrow gap is a model-facing/on-demand status response for approved program IDs *not yet enrolled in the reviewed app catalog*. If needed, expose bounded observations via the **existing stronger catalog**, never build another PATH scanner or let file existence imply runtime readiness/execution authority. |
| **OCR fallback (#18)** | `native_ocr.rs` already implements SHA-pinned local Tesseract/language data, bounded image/crop and selected-PDF-page OCR through reviewed rasterization, cancellation and process custody; the desktop host has native consent/dialog, attachment, region and PDF routes and stores **unverified** OCR digests. But `native_reader_tool.rs` explicitly rejects scanned image/PDF OCR in the ordinary model-visible Reader; OCR is a *separate operator-approved desktop path*. Old `native_ocr_fallback.rs` added a vision-verified-vs-OCR routing helper and bounded Tesseract TSV word-confidence/word-count parsing, absent from the current plain-text OCR response. | **OCR IS IMPLEMENTED; DO NOT IMPORT A DUPLICATE TESSERACT TOOL.** Optional distinct gaps are a reviewed Reader-to-existing-OCR admission path, confidence/provenance metadata, and automatic fallback only if explicit human-turn and worker grants remain enforced. No claim that current Reader itself automatically OCRs scanned PDFs. |
| **Native-tool and streaming generation loops (#19)** | `agent_tool_loop.rs` already guards repeated identical request/result pairs (threshold 3), denies repeated write effects, caps each turn at **32 tool steps** and fail-closes no-progress; `supervised_tool_generation.rs` records `LOOP_GUARD_STOP`, enforces cancellation, typed dispatch, finalization and bounded pre-dispatch regeneration. `generation_queue.rs`, `stream_delivery.rs` and llama transport limit bytes, tokens and elapsed time, but inspected stream delivery/llama transport do **not** show the old periodic-text cycle detector. Historical `loop_guard.rs` additionally classified **semantic** new read data and verified before/after write states across differing requests, warned/blocked alternating stalled calls, and permitted a new-approach recovery; historical `generation_loop_guard.rs` stopped repeated long text suffixes/empty streaming chunks. | **BASE PROTECTION IMPLEMENTED; NONDUPLICATE GUARD FEATURES REMAIN.** Preserve current authority, durable effect journal, OS custody and cancellation. Review incorporating only cross-call semantic-progress witnesses and chunk-stream repetition detection into the *existing* native turn/stream path, without reinstating legacy dispatcher or automatic privileges. Evaluate 32-step cap against proven long useful workflows rather than removing all limits. |

**Next action priority:** (1) retain current ANSF/Reader/OCR/loop implementations as owners, record revision-aware recall and streaming/cross-call progress as narrowly scoped source-equivalence checks; (2) design/implement the genuinely absent general-purpose JSON Schema validator through current typed native tool contracts; (3) assess whether a separate on-demand program presence probe is actually necessary given reviewed app inventory; (4) batch one integration-group test matrix at the final candidate SHA, not per edit. **No code, candidate ref or PR changed in this source-comparison step.** This ledger records intended integration, not accepted functionality.

**Hard exclusions:** Lu IDE remains a separate consolidation workstream; experimental P7L/KoboldCpp branches do **not** enter Lu Core, whose sole production inference backend is llama.cpp. Optional PERF-01 P6 multi-GPU cluster work stays deferred. First-party DLC interoperability contracts can be reviewed without importing specialist IDE/NVP runtimes.

**Execution rule:** Consolidate **unique source functionality**, not individual bugs or all historical PRs wholesale. Keep working on independent source tasks while CI runs; batch acceptance when a coherent final source head is ready. Neither green source verification nor a reviewed branch inventory authorizes an installer, native worker grant, main source merge or release. Preserve the existing automated open-PR metadata block below; its counts are a periodically refreshed snapshot, not this execution ledger.

---
## 1. Status rules (never collapse these)

| Evidence level | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| **Designed** | A requirement or interface exists; no runnable implementation is asserted. |
| **Draft source** | Exact code exists on a named branch/PR but may not be present on `main`. |
| **Compiled/tested** | Specific CI checks passed on the exact head SHA; **list failing/skipped jobs too**. |
| **Desktop integrated** | The production Tauri host/UI can reach that implementation at an exact compiled revision; static registration is not sufficient. |
| **Model exercised** | Real local GGUF/model used the tool in a representative authorized task. |
| **Installed accepted** | Actual installed Windows build executed the owner-approved acceptance scenario with recorded binary/source identity. |
| **Released** | An immutable tagged/distributed release with traceable artifacts, separate from development acceptance. |

No branch status, passing isolated test, code comment, diagram, or prior chat can promote an item to another level. Record **what was tested, where, and at which exact SHA**. Mark unknown results unknown. A successful earlier alpha does not confer acceptance on later binaries.

## 2. Accepted reference and current scope

- **Functional development alpha:** `0.1.0-alpha.2`, owner-accepted after physical Windows tests of GGUF/MMProj, chat, persistence, streaming, cancellation, queueing and Retry. See [acceptance record](LU_ALPHA_01_ACCEPTED_BASELINE_20261103.md). It is **not** an accepted native-tools v1.0 build, and its exact installer-to-`main` source equivalence is not asserted here.
- **Repository baseline observed at initial reconciliation (2026-11-05):** `main` SHA `6645f7939b60700c4012d3a2a18b7553480980a6`. This is a reference for the snapshot, **not** a permanent current-main claim.
- **Owner-approved order (2026-11-05):** consolidate and finish **general-purpose native tools** first; then connect ANSF/SQLite, operational memory, task checkpoints and host-owned relevant context assembly; finally complete v1.0 whole-app acceptance.
- **Out of Core scope:** specialized IDE debugging/GitHub workflows, NVP/Film video production, a duplicate research engine, and bespoke video/transcription engines. TOOLS-10 already owns native browser and research. Specialized engines/DLCs can use approved Core supervision later.

- **[Unified draft PR #34](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/pull/34)**, branch `lu/tools-01-05-unified-integration-20261105`, latest repair candidate `db3e55721de25b96328c78f10fd453dc000f6f91` (previous tested snapshot `a6c7153988644ccf6867b294ea17b14e61cabab1`). Based on existing #24 development branch; imported 30 unchanged Reader/background/ZIP source files from development PRs #27 and #15 by blob SHA, then reconciled Core Protobuf and modules, Rust/Windows worker ABI, 3 conflicting Tauri/React/chat-journal files, and added one combined native/tool UI CI matrix.
- **Confirmed at this exact head**: [Lu Desktop native-core tests](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/actions/runs/42092433475/job/127473685280) **passed**; [source identity](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/actions/runs/42092433475/job/127473684756) **passed**; [full Core+desktop Cargo.lock graph audit](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/actions/runs/42092433718/job/127473692113) **passed**. These verify unified source and native Core compilation/tests, **not** an installed release.
- **Integration additions:** `ModelToolCatalog::absorb_registered` atomically verifies independently registered native tool domains against the actual registry; combined Reader + read-only ZIP + background status/receipt allowlist regression at `src-tauri/lu-core/tests/unified_native_catalog.rs`. Tool registration is not an effect grant; model output never supplies arbitrary shell/PID or approved file path.
- **Real CI fixes, not skipped tests:** full first matrix found missing Git-test host-executable environment, Windows running a Linux PCI filename test, missing nonfunctional Tauri compile resources, and frontend npm-without-lock handling. Exact-current-branch CI uses the known Lu CI setup and `#[cfg(unix)]` on Linux-specific PCI fixture. Source-locked Windows native/desktop and frontend validation **pending** at ledger update; [run](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/actions/runs/42092433718).
- **Full previous matrix at `a6c7153`**: Windows Tauri compile, Linux native, native user-turn dispatch, chat journal, source identity, lockfile graph and diagnostics passed. The Windows native suite failed only on test cleanup of an open file-root handle; `npm run check` used the pre-background string assertion; scanner worker e2e lacked a built worker and had a cwd-dependent duplicated path. Repair commits `8c8f40a` (test/CI code), `5002b0c` (workflow directory), and `db3e557` (exact source manifest) fix those issues without relaxing tool authorization. [Exact candidate Actions run](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/actions/runs/42113888428) was **queued**, not verified, at this ledger update.
- **Important model-path limit:** ordinary desktop `lu_generate` still uses the inference supervisor directly; the separately approved `Ask Reader` path has a supervised model tool turn, but the ZIP/Git Core registrations are not yet wired to that ordinary desktop path. Source/CI success must not be reported as all five tools autonomously operational with a real GGUF.
- **Open acceptance**: complete full platform CI on exact SHA, correct any merge-caused desktop defects, actual real-GGUF model/tool-loop and recovery checks, trusted verified artifact manifests, Reader real files and scanned/OCR support as approved, physical Windows installed NSIS acceptance. Preserve accepted `0.1.0-alpha.2`; **PR #34 is draft, not merged into main**. This is not reason to rebuild any already completed core native tools.

## 5. Integration constraints and next handoff

1. **One integration head at a time.** Choose a source-locked integration branch anchored to an exact `main` commit; do not blindly merge all draft branches. Track intersecting files (`src-tauri/lu-core/src/lib.rs`, `core.proto`, desktop `lib.rs`, React `main.jsx`, Cargo manifests and lockfiles).
2. **Batch closely related tool functionality, not redundant implementations.** Verify a coherent tool family with full native checks and desktop dispatch before promoting it. Preserve source-only branches for provenance; safely close superseded PRs **only after** their unique code/evidence is accounted for.
3. **Current technical blockers:** existing Windows worker Job API discrepancy, mixed CI results, absent pinned browser runtime, Reader PDF/OCR limitations and unsynchronized desktop integrations. These are not grounds to declare all tools unbuilt.
4. **Next development gate:** tool branch/source/proto reconciliation → exact-head CI → real GGUF authorized tool loop → Windows installer acceptance; **then** memory integration per the owner decision. The accepted functional alpha remains unchanged.
5. **No faux release:** Update [CHANGELOG.md](../CHANGELOG.md) only when an owner-accepted milestone/release is established. A source commit or draft PR goes in this ledger, not into released-version notes.

## 6. Change-control workflow (mandatory for future Lu work)

- **Before writing:** read this `main` ledger, [CHANGELOG.md](../CHANGELOG.md), the affected PRs and current **exact head SHAs**; check the target branch and PR base. A conversation summary alone is not the branch status.
- **For each feature PR:** put a compact implementation receipt in the PR description: `LU-ID`, changed paths, original/base/head SHA, source/runtime scope, tests **passed/failed/skipped**, integration and installed-state truth, dependencies and next gate. Use [PR template](../.github/pull_request_template.md). Prefer independent PRs against `main`; use stacked branches only with an explicit dependency and plan to rebase/retarget.
- **When code changes:** update the relevant PR receipt and explicitly state what remains untested. Do **not** record `DONE` merely because a new source file exists or isolated tests pass.
- **At integration/merge:** update this feature map and acceptance/CI evidence on `main` in the same integration pass; add to release changelog **only** after acceptance. Avoid editing the central narrative independently in 20 competing feature branches: keep pending progress in the PR receipt and auto-generated inventory.
- **To refresh PR table:** `node scripts/lu-ledger.mjs --update`; `--check` detects drift. An optional GitHub Actions scheduled refresh updates **only** the marked table, never the manual evidence/status sections. GitHub API metadata cannot certify correctness of code.
- **End of each development session:** report current PR/commit, actual tests, failures, what's now in main, and next concrete gate. This is the handoff to the next conversation.
